`are you able to start 018?` through the export cutoff. Earlier S2-010 and Sprint 1
chat, internal reasoning, system/developer messages and binary attachments are
outside this task-scoped excerpt. Original failed commands and corrections are
retained. Local home-directory paths are replaced with `<USER_HOME>`. Nothing is
reconstructed or invented; this is not a claim to export the entire continuing chat.

and late-response tests, and a proposed integration handoff. S2-010 remains in its
original worktree. No shared UI/endpoint or model contracts have been changed.
Pending: actual context/model, summary and UI integration; shared agreement,
human/security reviews, browser acceptance and PR CI/merge. This foundation is not
full acceptance of S2-018 or S2-019.

### 2026-11-12T18:57:36.803Z ? assistant

S2-018 also needs Melissa’s UI and Bansi’s summary to follow the selected target. I’ve created a separate worktree for it, leaving S2-010 untouched.

I’ll start with a reusable request coordinator: changing targets will clear the old answer, abort pending browser work and reject late responses. This lets us test switching behavior before the shared Q&A endpoint and UI contracts are finalized.

  "name": "exec",
  "input": "const source = `export interface TargetRequestScope {\n  /** Non-secret UI session identity/epoch. Never put an auth token here. */\n  readonly sessionKey: string;\n  readonly targetId: string;\n}\n\nexport interface TargetRequestTicket extends TargetRequestScope {\n  readonly signal: AbortSignal;\n}\n\nexport interface TargetResult<T> {\n  readonly targetId: string;\n  readonly value: T;\n}\n\nexport interface TargetRequestSnapshot<T> {\n  readonly scope: TargetRequestScope | null;\n  readonly status: \"idle\" | \"pending\" | \"ready\" | \"error\";\n  readonly result: TargetResult<T> | null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Local request lifecycle for ONE workspace instance. No shared store, review cache, auth\n * decision or HTTP/model contract lives here. The caller must authorize every server request.\n */\nexport function createTargetRequestCoordinator<T>() {\n  let disposed = false;\n  let scope: TargetRequestScope | null = null;\n  let current: { ticket: TargetRequestTicket; controller: AbortController } | null = null;\n  let snapshot: TargetRequestSnapshot<T> = Object.freeze({\n    scope: null,\n    status: \"idle\",\n    result: null,\n  });\n\n  function setSnapshot(status: TargetRequestSnapshot<T>[\"status\"], result: TargetResult<T> | null) {\n    snapshot = Object.freeze({ scope, status, result });\n    return snapshot;\n  }\n\n  function assertActive() {\n    if (disposed) throw new Error(\"The target request coordinator has been disposed.\");\n  }\n\n  function isCurrent(ticket: TargetRequestTicket): boolean {\n    return (\n      !disposed &&\n      current?.ticket === ticket &&\n      !ticket.signal.aborted &&\n      scope?.targetId === ticket.targetId &&\n      scope?.sessionKey === ticket.sessionKey\n    );\n  }\n\n  function selectTarget(next: TargetRequestScope | null): TargetRequestSnapshot<T> {\n    assertActive();\n    if (\n      next &&\n      (typeof next.targetId !== \"string\" ||\n        !next.targetId.trim() ||\n        typeof next.sessionKey !== \"string\" ||\n        !next.sessionKey.trim())\n    ) {\n      throw new Error(\"A target and non-secret session key are required.\");\n    }\n    if (scope?.targetId === next?.targetId && scope?.sessionKey === next?.sessionKey) return snapshot;\n    const previous = current;\n    current = null;\n    scope = next ? Object.freeze({ sessionKey: next.sessionKey, targetId: next.targetId }) : null;\n    // Clear old content BEFORE notifying abort listeners, which may run synchronously.\n    setSnapshot(\"idle\", null);\n    previous?.controller.abort();\n    return snapshot;\n  }\n\n  function beginRequest(): TargetRequestTicket {\n    assertActive();\n    if (!scope) throw new Error(\"Select a target before starting a request.\");\n    const previous = current;\n    const controller = new AbortController();\n    const ticket: TargetRequestTicket = Object.freeze({ ...scope, signal: controller.signal });\n    current = { ticket, controller };\n    setSnapshot(\"pending\", null);\n    previous?.controller.abort();\n    return ticket;\n  }\n\n  function acceptResult(ticket: TargetRequestTicket, response: TargetResult<T>): boolean {\n    if (!isCurrent(ticket)) return false;\n    const previous = current;\n    current = null;\n    if (response.targetId !== ticket.targetId) {\n      setSnapshot(\"error\", null);\n      previous?.controller.abort();\n      return false;\n    }\n    setSnapshot(\"ready\", Object.freeze({ targetId: response.targetId, value: response.value }));\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  function failRequest(ticket: TargetRequestTicket): boolean {\n    if (!isCurrent(ticket)) return false;\n    current = null;\n    // UI chooses a safe message from the agreed API response. Never store arbitrary thrown errors.\n    setSnapshot(\"error\", null);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  function cancelRequest(): TargetRequestSnapshot<T> {\n    assertActive();\n    const previous = current;\n    current = null;\n    setSnapshot(\"idle\", null);\n    previous?.controller.abort();\n    return snapshot;\n  }\n\n  function dispose() {\n    if (disposed) return;\n    disposed = true;\n    const previous = current;\n    current = null;\n    scope = null;\n    setSnapshot(\"idle\", null);\n    previous?.controller.abort();\n  }\n\n  return {\n    selectTarget,\n    beginRequest,\n    acceptResult,\n    failRequest,\n    cancelRequest,\n    dispose,\n    isCurrent,\n    getSnapshot: () => snapshot,\n  };\n}\n`;\nconst tests = `/** @jest-environment node */\nimport { createTargetRequestCoordinator, type TargetRequestScope } from \"@/lib/qa/target-request-coordinator\";\n\nconst A: TargetRequestScope = { sessionKey: \"user-a-session\", targetId: \"target-a\" };\nconst B: TargetRequestScope = { sessionKey: \"user-a-session\", targetId: \"target-b\" };\nconst OTHER_USER: TargetRequestScope = { sessionKey: \"user-b-session\", targetId: \"target-a\" };\n\nit(\"uses the selected target for subsequent questions and keeps distinguishable evidence separate\", async () => {\n  const coordinator = createTargetRequestCoordinator<string>();\n  const persistedEvidence = new Map([\n    [A.targetId, \"ALPHA: battery life\"],\n    [B.targetId, \"BETA: customer service\"],\n  ]);\n  const modelInputs: { targetId: string; evidence: string }[] = [];\n  // Controlled consumer, not the unimplemented production Q&A endpoint/model boundary.\n  async function ask() {\n    const ticket = coordinator.beginRequest();\n    const evidence = persistedEvidence.get(ticket.targetId)!;\n    modelInputs.push({ targetId: ticket.targetId, evidence });\n    coordinator.acceptResult(ticket, { targetId: ticket.targetId, value: evidence });\n  }\n  coordinator.selectTarget(A);\n  await ask();\n  expect(coordinator.getSnapshot().result?.value).toContain(\"battery life\");\n  coordinator.selectTarget(B);\n  expect(coordinator.getSnapshot()).toMatchObject({ scope: B, status: \"idle\", result: null });\n  await ask();\n  expect(modelInputs).toEqual([\n    { targetId: A.targetId, evidence: \"ALPHA: battery life\" },\n    { targetId: B.targetId, evidence: \"BETA: customer service\" },\n  ]);\n  expect(coordinator.getSnapshot().result?.value).not.toContain(\"ALPHA\");\n});\n\nit(\"clears an existing answer immediately when the target changes\", () => {\n  const coordinator = createTargetRequestCoordinator<string>();\n  coordinator.selectTarget(A);\n  const a = coordinator.beginRequest();\n  expect(coordinator.acceptResult(a, { targetId: A.targetId, value: \"A answer\" })).toBe(true);\n  const previous = coordinator.getSnapshot();\n  coordinator.selectTarget(B);\n  expect(coordinator.getSnapshot().result).toBeNull();\n  expect(previous.result?.value).toBe(\"A answer\");\n});\n\nit(\"aborts A but does not depend on abort preventing its late response\", () => {\n  const coordinator = createTargetRequestCoordinator<string>();\n  coordinator.selectTarget(A);\n  const a = coordinator.beginRequest();\n  coordinator.selectTarget(B);\n  expect(a.signal.aborted).toBe(true);\n  const b = coordinator.beginRequest();\n  expect(coordinator.acceptResult(a, { targetId: A.targetId, value: \"late A\" })).toBe(false);\n  expect(coordinator.getSnapshot().status).toBe(\"pending\");\n  expect(coordinator.acceptResult(b, { targetId: B.targetId, value: \"B answer\" })).toBe(true);\n  expect(coordinator.getSnapshot().result?.value).toBe(\"B answer\");\n});\n\nit(\"rejects an old A ticket even after A-to-B-to-A\", () => {\n  const coordinator = expect(coordinator.getSnapshot().status).toBe(\"pending\");\n});\n\nit(\"rejects a response tagged with the wrong target without displaying its value\", () => {\n  const coordinator = createTargetRequestCoordinator<string>();\n  coordinator.selectTarget(B);\n  const b = coordinator.beginRequest();\n  expect(coordinator.acceptResult(b, { targetId: A.targetId, value: \"PRIVATE A\" })).toBe(false);\n  expect(coordinator.getSnapshot()).toMatchObject({ scope: B, status: \"error\", result: null });\n});\n\nit(\"invalidates pending work when the account/session changes, even for the same target ID\", () => {\n  const coordinator = createTargetRequestCoordinator<string>();\n  coordinator.selectTarget(A);\n  const a = coordinator.beginRequest();\n  coordinator.selectTarget(OTHER_USER);\n  expect(a.signal.aborted).toBe(true);\n  expect(coordinator.getSnapshot().result).toBeNull();\n  expect(coordinator.acceptResult(a, { targetId: A.targetId, ### 2026-11-12T19:00:00.122Z ? custom_tool_call

{
  "type": "custom_tool_call",
  "id": "ctc_0d111573f83b7797016af60c93ef1487d1aa49c3805f2416b9",
  "status": "completed",
  "call_id": "call_d7a4aedfa847422a874ba8e6f9f6c4f7",
  "name": "exec",
  "input": "const doc=`# S2-018: Target-scoped Q&A request switching\n\nIssue: [#69](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/issues/69).\nOwner: Shawn. Status: **independent implementation foundation**, not full story\nacceptance. The shared API/UI contract is still proposed.\n\n## Scope and ownership\n\n\\`src/lib/qa/target-request-coordinator.ts\\` owns local request/result invalidation\nfor one workspace. It is generic over response content, so it does not define\nnormal-answer/refusal/insufficient-evidence HTTP shapes, system prompts or summary\nmetrics. Melissa retains ownership of the workspace and switching/Q&A UI; Aryan\nowns the endpoint/model; Bansi owns the summary. None of their files are changed.\n\nThis branch is based independently on main \\`f931a34\\`. It does not depend on or\ncopy the uncommitted S2-010 work. S2-010's fresh session-authorized context builder\nwill need to be integrated into Aryan's real Q&A request separately.\n\n## Proposed local integration contract\n\nCreate one coordinator per workspace/session lifetime. Never export a global\nsingleton. A scope contains the active \\`targetId\\` and a non-secret \\`sessionKey\\`\nthat changes on logout/account changes; this key is local UI isolation, **not**\nauthentication. It must never contain a JWT, cookie, password or provider key.\n\n| Method | Effect |\n| --- | --- |\n| \\`selectTarget(scope)\\` | Copies the scope; on change immediately clears result/error state and aborts pending work |\n| \\`selectTarget(null)\\` | Clears active state for deselection/logout |\n| \\`beginRequest()\\` | Captures current target/session in an opaque ticket, marks pending and invalidates the previous request |\n| \\`acceptResult(ticket, { targetId, value })\\` | Accepts once only if this instance/request/scope is still current and the response target matches |\n| \\`failRequest(ticket)\\` | Shows error state only for a current request; ignores stale failures |\n| \\`cancelRequest()\\` | Clears work/result while retaining the selected scope |\n| \\`getSnapshot()\\` | Returns scope, status and the accepted target-tagged result, or null |\n| \\`dispose()\\` | Aborts/clears and permanently invalidates this instance |\n| \\`isCurrent(ticket)\\` | Allows adapters to check scope before side effects |\n\nThe returned ticket provides \\`targetId\\`, \\`sessionKey\\` and an \\`AbortSignal\\`.\nAdapters must use the ticket's captured target for submission rather than read a\nlater mutable current-target variable. A ticket cannot be reconstructed from a\ntarget ID; object identity distinguishes old A from new A after A-to-B-to-A.\n\nThe \\`TargetResult<T>\\` wrapper is a proposed **local adapter contract**, not a\nchange to the endpoint response. The network response's target identity must be\nvalidated/adapted after the shared API contract is agreed. A wrong-target current\nresponse is discarded and becomes a safe error state, not an answer. Arbitrary\nthrown error objects/messages are not stored here.\n\nExample consumer flow (pseudocode, not the unimplemented production endpoint):\n\n1. Select the authenticated UI session and current target.\n2. Begin a request; send its target ID and question, with its signal.\n3. Server independently authenticates/authorizes and calls the fresh S2-010\n   builder for that requested target before any model invocation.\n4. Validate/adapt the agreed response and pass the ticket plus target-tagged\n   result to \\`acceptResult\\`.\n5. Publish only the coordinator's current snapshot to the UI. Do not render a\n   rejected raw promise result or update UI unconditionally in \\`finally\\`.\n6. Use \\`failRequest(ticket)\\` for errors. A previous request's error/cleanup must\n   not reset the new target's answer/loading state.\n7. On a target/session change invalidate the scope immediately; on final unmount\n   dispose of the instance.\n\n## Integration invariants to agree with Melissa/Aryan/Bansi\n\n- The visible target, summary, question request and answer must all use the same\n  current target ID. Summary loads also need target-tagged correlation; this\n  coordinator does not calculate or load summary data.\n- Clear/hide previous-target content before it can paint for the new target.\n  A Q&A subtree keyed by target/session is one possible integration, but pending\n  responses still need correlation. Do not rely solely on a delayed effect.\n- React Strict Mode may replay effect cleanup/setup. An adapter must create a new\n  coordinator for a new lifetime; it cannot reuse a disposed instance.\n- Abort is best-effort browser cancellation. It does not establish server/model\n  cancellation, eliminate paid work or replace identity checks. Late responses\n  are rejected even if the transport ignores abort.\n- Fresh server-side context and no reused provider conversation history are\n  required. This frontend helper cannot prove those boundaries by itself.\n- Same-scope reselection is a no-op; a new question supersedes old work. Returning\n  to A after B clears prior A data rather than restoring a cached answer.\n- The UI may display any agreed answer category through generic \\`T\\`, but no\n  category is allowed to bypass target/session correlation.\n- Logout must select null/dispose; account changes must change the non-secret\n  session key, even if the selected target ID happens to be the same.\n\n## Tests and remaining acceptance\n\n\\`tests/unit/lib/qa/target-request-coordinator.test.ts\\` uses distinct Alpha/Beta\nfixtures, captures a controlled consumer's model-input values and resolves\npromises in reverse order. It covers switching, A-to-B-to-A, latest request wins,\nlate success/failure, wrong-target responses, user-session changes, cancellation,\ndisposal, separate instances, duplicate completion, scope copying and abort\nobserver order.\n\nThis is **not** the production Q&A model boundary or a browser test. No claim is\nmade that model history, real UI rendering or the active summary is integrated.\nThose require the actual S2-009/011/017 and summary work plus agreed contracts.\nS2-019 still needs its full stale-context audit across browser/server/model/cache\nboundaries; this helper provides shared local invalidation groundwork only.\n\nBefore completing #69: integrate the adapter, make the UI and summary visibly\nfollow B, inspect the actual outgoing model context to exclude A, run transition\nbrowser/integration tests and all configured gates, obtain human/security reviews\nand PR CI, and retain original AI dialogue. No paid call or hosted mutation is\nneeded for deterministic tests.\n`;\nstore(\"s2018Doc\",doc);\nconst base=\"C:\\\\Users\\\\shawn\\\\Documents\\\\NJIT\\\\Fall 26\\\\CS490\\\\ReviewLens-Codex-Artifacts\\\\reviewlens-s2-018-target-context\";\nconst cmd=`$taskFile = '${base}\\\\docs\\\\s2-018-target-context-switching.md'\\n$taskContents = @'\\n${doc}\\n'@\\n[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($taskFile, $taskContents, [System.Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))\\nnpm.cmd exec -- prettier --write docs/s2-018-target-context-switching.md\\nnpm.cmd run typecheck`;\ntext(await tools.exec_command({cmd,workdir:base,sandbox_permissions:\"require_escalated\",justification:\"May I save the S2-018 integration proposal and run typecheck in its separate worktree?\",yield_time_ms:1000,max_output_tokens:1800}));\n",
  "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {
    "turn_id": "01a1d11d-6390-74d0-9dee-95792def34bf",
    "create_time": 1794509971.9372954
  }
}

Next.js\\r\\nnav_title: Data Security\\r\\ndescription: Learn the built-in data security features in Next.js and learn best practices for protecting your application's data.\\r\\nrelated:\\r\\n  title: Next Steps\\r\\n  description: Learn more about the topics mentioned in this guide.\\r\\n  links:\\r\\n    - app/guides/authentication\\r\\n    - app/guides/content-security-policy\\r\\n    - app/guides/forms\\r\\n    - app/guides/server-actions\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n[React Server Components](https://react.dev/reference/rsc/server-components) improve performance and simplify data fetching, but also shift where and how data is accessed, changing some of the traditional security assumptions for handling data in frontend apps.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis guide will help you understand how to think about data security in Next.js and how to implement best practices.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Data fetching approaches\\r\\n\\r\\nThere are three main approaches we recommend for fetching data in Next.js, depending on the size and age of your project:\\r\\n\\r\\n- [HTTP APIs](#external-http-apis): for existing large applications and organizations.\\r\\n- [Data Access Layer](#data-access-layer): for new projects.\\r\\n- [Component-Level Data Access](#component-level-data-access): for prototypes and learning.\\r\\n\\r\\nWe recommend choosing one data fetching approach and avoiding mixing them. This makes it clear for both developers working in your code base and security auditors what to expect.\\r\\n\\r\\n### External HTTP APIs\\r\\n\\r\\nYou should follow a **Zero Trust** model when adopting Server Components in an existing project. You can continue calling your existing API endpoints such as REST or GraphQL from Server Components using [`fetch`](/docs/app/api-reference/functions/fetch), just as you would in Client Components.\\r\\n\\r\\n```tsx filename=\\\"app/page.tsx\\\"\\r\\nimport { cookies } from 'next/headers'\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default async function Page() {\\r\\n  const cookieStore = await cookies()\\r\\n  const token = cookieStore.get('AUTH_TOKEN')?.value\\r\\n\\r\\n  const res = await fetch('https://api.example.com/profile', {\\r\\n    headers: {\\r\\n      Cookie: `AUTH_TOKEN=${token}`,\\r\\n      // Other headers\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  })\\r\\n\\r\\n  // ....\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nThis approach works well when:\\r\\n\\r\\n- You already have security practices in place.\\r\\n- Separate backend teams use other languages or manage APIs independently.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Data Access Layer\\r\\n\\r\\nFor new projects, we recommend creating a dedicated **Data Access Layer (DAL)**. This is an internal library that controls how and when data is fetched, and what gets passed to your render context.\\r\\n\\r\\nA Data Access Layer should:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Only run on the server.\\r\\n- Perform authorization checks.\\r\\n- Return safe, minimal **Data Transfer Objects (DTOs)**.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis approach centralizes all data access logic, making it easier to enforce consistent data access and reduces the risk of authorization bugs. You also get the benefit of sharing an in-memory cache across different parts of a request.\\r\\n\\r\\n```ts filename=\\\"data/auth.ts\\\"\\r\\nimport { cache } from 'react'\\r\\nimport { cookies } from 'next/headers'\\r\\n\\r\\n// Cached helper methods makes it easy to get the same value in many places\\r\\n// without manually passing it around. This discourages passing it from Server\\r\\n// Component to Server Component which minimizes risk of passing it to a Client\\r\\n// Component.\\r\\nexport const getCurrentUser = cache(async () => {\\r\\n  const cookieStore = await cookies()\\r\\n  const token = cookieStore.get('AUTH_TOKEN')\\r\\n  const decodedToken = await decryptAndValidate(token)\\r\\n  // Don't include secret tokens or private information as public fields.\\r\\n  // Use classes to avoid accidentally passing the whole object to the client.\\r\\n  return new User(decodedToken.id)\\r\\n})\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n```tsx filename=\\\"data/user-dto.tsx\\\"\\r\\nimport 'server-only'\\r\\nimport { getCurrentUser } from './auth'\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction canSeeUsername(viewer: User) {\\r\\n\"}"

Build authenticated review dataset context for Q&A\\\"},{\\\"url\\\":\\\"https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/pull/77\\\",\\\"title\\\":\\\"S2-015: Entity and Platform Refusal Evaluation Foundation (Phase 1)\\\",\\\"issue_number\\\":77,\\\"body\\\":\\\"## Summary\\\\n**Phase 1 foundation** for S2-015 — Reject Questions About Another Entity or Platform (issue (`SYSTEM_PROMPT_VERSION = \\\\\\\"s2-013.v1\\\\\\\"`), `buildSystemPrompt`, `buildQaModelRequest`, the `QA_OUTCOMES` response contract, and `QaPromptError`\\\\n- `tests/unit/services/qa/system-prompt.test.ts`: 18 Jest tests, 100% coverage of the new module\\\\n- `docs/s2-013-system-prompt.md`: requirement-to-test mapping, model response contract, safety guards, and what S2-010 / S2-011 / S2-003 / S2-009 need from this module\\\\n- `src/services/README.md`: new `qa/` row\\\\n\\\\nRefs #64\\\\n\\\\n## S2-013 requirements\\\\n\\\\n| Requirement | Where |\\\\n| --- | --- |\\\\n| Names the product/entity being analyzed | Active dataset section (target name, JSON-encoded) |\\\\n| Names the review platform | Active dataset section (`google_maps` → \\\\\\\"Google Maps\\\\\\\") |\\\\n| Answers based only on the supplied reviews | Evidence rules 1–2 |\\\\n| Unsupported information is not invented | Evidence rules 3–5 (also covers S2-012) |\\\\n| General-knowledge questions are declined | Scope rule 6 (S2-014) |\\\\n| Other product/entity/platform questions are declined | Scope rules 7–8 (S2-015) |\\\\n| Prompt kept in source control | `src/services/qa/system-prompt.ts`, versioned, reviewed via PR |\\\\n\\\\nEach row has a test in `system-prompt.test.ts`.\\\\n\\\\n## Model response contract\\\\n\\\\nThe model replies with only `{\\\\\\\"outcome\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"answer\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"citedReviewIds\\\\\\\"}`. The outcomes map one-to-one onto the S2-004 evaluation categories (#72):\\\\n\\\\n| `outcome` | S2-004 category |\\\\n| --- | --- |\\\\n| `answered` | `answer_from_active_reviews` |\\\\n| `insufficient_evidence` | `insufficient_review_evidence` |\\\\n| `declined_general_knowledge` | `decline_unrelated_general_knowledge` |\\\\n| `declined_other_entity` | `decline_other_entity_or_product` |\\\\n| `declined_other_platform` | `decline_other_review_platform` |\\\\n\\\\nParsing and validating the reply is S2-011's job and is not in this PR.\\\\n\\\\n## Security notes (for the S1-004 §7 security review)\\\\n\\\\nThis PR defines what data is sent to the AI provider, so it needs the additional security review for AI-data-boundary and third-party-call changes (engineering standards §12, AI-Assisted Development §7).\\\\n\\\\n- **No empty-target requests:** `buildQaModelRequest` throws `NO_REVIEW_DATA` for an empty dataset, so an empty target can never become a request the model could answer from general knowledge (S2-020).\\\\n- **Wrong-target backstop:** it throws `REVIEW_TARGET_MISMATCH` if any review's `analysisTargetId` differs from the active target. The error message does not include review content. This backs up S2-010's ownership checks; it does not replace them (S2-016, S2-019).\\\\n- **Data minimization:** only review `id`, `rating`, `reviewDate`, and `text` are sent. Reviewer names, review URLs, and source metadata are never sent (tested with marker values).\\\\n- **Prompt injection:** the target name, review text, and question are JSON-encoded, so they cannot close or forge a prompt section (tested). The prompt also tells the model to treat them as data, not instructions.\\\\n- **Server only:** the module imports `server-only`, so the prompt is not shipped to the browser.\\\\n- **No new dependencies, no provider calls, no secrets.**\\\\n\\\\n## Not in this PR\\\\n\\\\n- Loading and selecting reviews (S2-010, Shawn)\\\\n- Calling the OpenAI API and parsing the reply (S2-011)\\\\n- Mock model harness (S2-003, Deep)\\\\n- Q&A route and question length limit (route owner, S2-009)\\\\n\\\\n## Open item\\\\n\\\\nThe S2-BR rule text is not in the repository. If this document conflicts with an active sprint requirement, the active sprint requirement takes priority and this document must be updated through a reviewed pull request.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis is a living team document. Approved Technology Baseline\\r\\n\\r\\n### Frontend\\r\\n\\r\\n- Next.js App Router\\r\\n- React\\r\\n- TypeScript in strict mode\\r\\n- Tailwind CSS\\r\\n- shadcn/ui\\r\\n\\r\\n### Backend\\r\\n\\r\\n- Next.js Route Handlers\\r\\n- Server-side TypeScript services\\r\\n\\r\\n### Database and Authentication\\r\\n\\r\\n- Supabase PostgreSQL\\r\\n- Supabase Auth\\r\\n- Supabase Row Level Security (RLS) as an additional database-level authorization layer\\r\\n\\r\\n### Testing\\r\\n\\r\\n- Jest\\r\\n- React Testing Library\\r\\n- Playwright\\r\\n\\r\\n### Delivery and External Services\\r\\n\\r\\n- npm as the only package manager\\r\\n- GitHub Actions for continuous integration\\r\\n- Vercel for application deployment\\r\\n- Supabase hosted services for authentication and persistence\\r\\n- OpenAI API for later review-analysis functionality\\r\\n- Review provider/API pending final team and professor approval\\r\\n\\r\\nThe exact supported versions and generated configuration are established by S1-005 and S1-006 and must be recorded in the repository lockfile and setup documentation.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 3. throw new Error(\\\"Enter a question about the reviews.\\\");\\n    }\\n    const ticket = coordinator.beginRequest();\\n    publish();\\n    // A subscriber may synchronously select another target while pending is published.\\n    if (!coordinator.isCurrent(ticket)) return false;\\n    try {\\n      const response = await transport.execute({\\n        targetId: ticket.targetId,\\n        question: question.trim(),\\n        signal: ticket.signal,\\n      });\\n      const accepted = coordinator.acceptResult(ticket, response);\\n      publish();\\n      return accepted;\\n    } catch {\\n      // Do not retain arbitrary exceptions, provider diagnostics or response bodies in UI state.\\n      coordinator.failRequest(ticket);\\n      publish();\\n      return false;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  function cancel() {\\n    coordinator.cancelRequest();\\n    publish();\\n  }\\n\\n  function subscribe(listener: () => void) {\\n    if (disposed) throw new Error(\\\"The target question session has been disposed.\\\");\\n    listeners.add(listener);\\n    return () => {\\n      listeners.delete(listener);\\n    };\\n  }\\n\\n  function dispose() {\\n    if (disposed) return;\\n    disposed = true;\\n    coordinator.dispose();\\n    publish();\\n    listeners.clear();\\n  }\\n\\n  return {\\n    selectTarget,\\n    ask,\\n    cancel,\\n    dispose,\\n    subscribe,\\n    getSnapshot: coordinator.getSnapshot,\\n  };\\n}\\n'@\\n[IO.File]::WriteAllText($taskFile, $taskContent, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))\",\"workdir\":\"<USER_HOME>\\\\Documents\\\\NJIT\\\\Fall Please try again.</p>}\\n    </>;\\n  }\\n  render(<Consumer />);\\n  return { session, pending };\\n}\\n\\nit(\\\"visibly switches to Beta and never renders Alpha's delayed answer\\\", async () => {\\n  const { session, pending } = setup();\\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(\\\"Ask\\\"));\\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(\\\"Select Beta\\\"));\\n  expect(screen.getByText(\\\"Active: beta\\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\n  expect(screen.getByText(\\\"Fixture summary: customer service\\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\n  expect(screen.queryByText(/battery life/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();\\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(\\\"Ask\\\"));\\n  expect(pending.map(item => item.request.targetId)).toEqual([\\\"alpha\\\", \\\"beta\\\"]);\\n  await act(async () => { pending[1].resolve({ targetId: \\\"beta\\\", value: \\\"BETA service evidence\\\" }); });\\n  expect(screen.getByText(\\\"Answer: BETA service evidence\\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\n  await act(async () => { pending[0].resolve({ targetId: \\\"alpha\\\", value: \\\"ALPHA battery secret\\\" }); });\\n  expect(screen.queryByText(/ALPHA/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();\\n  expect(screen.getByText(\\\"Answer: BETA service evidence\\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\n  act(() => session.dispose());\\n});\\n\\nit(\\\"removes an already-visible answer immediately on switching and ignores late completion after logout\\\", async () => {\\n  const { session, pending } = setup();\\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(\\\"Ask\\\"));\\n  await act(async () => { pending[0].resolve({ targetId: \\\"alpha\\\", value: \\\"ALPHA battery life\\\" }); });\\n  expect(screen.getByText(\\\"Answer: ALPHA battery life\\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(\\\"Select Beta\\\"));\\n  expect(screen.queryByText(/^Answer:/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();\\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(\\\"Ask\\\"));\\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(\\\"Sign out\\\"));\\n  expect(screen.getByText(\\\"Active: none\\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\n  expect(screen.getByText(\\\"Ask\\\")).toBeDisabled();\\n  await act(async () => { pending[1].resolve({ targetId: \\\"beta\\\", value: \\\"BETA late answer\\\" }); });\\n  expect(screen.queryByText(/^Answer:/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();\\n  expect(screen.queryByText(/Fixture summary/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();\\n  act(() => session.dispose());\\n});\\n\\nit(\\\"renders safe error feedback without displaying a mismatched target's data\\\", async () => {\\n  const { session, pending } = setup();\\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(\\\"Ask\\\"));\\n  await act(async () => { pending[0].resolve({ targetId: \\\"beta\\\", value: \\\"PRIVATE_OTHER_TARGET\\\" }); resolve: (value: string) => void }[] = [];\\n  const model = jest.fn((request: QaModelRequest) => new Promise<string>(resolve => {\\n    pending.push({ request, resolve });\\n  }));\\n  const session = createTargetQuestionSession<string>({ execute: async ({ targetId, question }) => {\\n    const context = await buildReviewDatasetContext(targetId);\\n    const request = buildQaModelRequest({ target: context.target, reviews: context.reviews, question });\\n    const answer = await model(request);\\n    return { targetId: request.targetId, value: answer };\\n  } });\\n  const select = (targetId: string, sessionKey = USER) => session.selectTarget({ targetId, sessionKey });\\n  return { session, select, model, pending, reviews };\\n}\\nbeforeEach(() => jest.resetAllMocks());\\n\\nasync function waitForCalls(model: ReturnType<typeof setup>[\\\"model\\\"], count: number) {\\n  for (let i = 0; i < 50 && model.mock.calls.length < count; i++) {\\n    await new Promise<void>(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));\\n  }\\n  expect(model).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(count);\\n}\\n\\nit(\\\"switches actual persisted-context/prompt inputs to Beta and discards a late Alpha model result\\\", async () => {\\n  const { session, select, model, pending } = setup();\\n  select(A);\\n  const alpha = session.ask(\\\"What do the reviews say?\\\");\\n  await waitForCalls(model, 1);\\n  select(B);\\n  const beta = session.ask(\\\"What do the reviews say?\\\");\\n  await waitForCalls(model, 2);\\n  const sent = JSON.stringify(pending[1].request);\\n  expect(sent).toContain(\\\"BETA_SERVICE_EVIDENCE\\\");\\n  expect(sent).not.toMatch(/ALPHA_BATTERY_EVIDENCE|a-review|Alpha bakery|OTHER_PRIVATE|OMIT_/);\\n  pending[1].resolve(\\\"BETA customer service\\\");\\n  expect(await beta).toBe(true);\\n  pending[0].resolve(\\\"ALPHA battery life\\\");\\n  expect(await alpha).toBe(false);\\n  expect(session.getSnapshot().result?.value).toBe(\\\"BETA customer service\\\");\\n});\\n\\nit(\\\"does not call the controlled model for a foreign target\\\", async () => {\\n  const { session, select, model } = setup();\\n  select(C);\\n  expect(await session.ask(\\\"Question\\\")).toBe(false);\\n  expect(model).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\\n  expect(session.getSnapshot()).toMatchObject({ status: \\\"error\\\", result: null });\\n});\\n\\nit(\\\"does not call the controlled model for an empty target\\\", async () => {\\n  const { session, select, model, reviews } = setup();\\n  reviews.splice(0, 1);\\n  select(A);\\n  expect(await session.ask(\\\"Question\\\")).toBe(false);\\n  expect(model).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\\n  expect(session.getSnapshot().result).toBeNull();\\n});\\n\\nit(\\\"does not call the controlled model for oversized complete evidence\\\", async () => {\\n  const { session, select, model, reviews } = setup();\\n  const base = reviews[0];\\n  reviews.splice(0, 1);\\n  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) reviews.push({ ...base, id: `large-${i}`, review_text: \\\"x\\\".repeat(9000) });\\n  expect(36000).toBeGreaterThan(MAX_CONTEXT_EVIDENCE_BYTES);\\n  select(A);\\n  expect(await session.ask(\\\"Question\\\")).toBe(false);\\n  expect(model).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\\n});\\n\\nit(\\\"uses fresh authenticated identity after account switching and rejects the old user's pending result\\\", async () => {\\n  const { session, select, model, pending } = setup();\\n  select(A);\\n  const alpha = session.ask(\\\"Question\\\");\\n  await waitForCalls(model, 1);\\n  jest.mocked(getAuthenticatedUser).mockResolvedValue({ id: OTHER, email: \\\"other@example.test\\\" });\\n  select(C, OTHER);\\n  const other = session.ask(\\\"Question\\\");\\n  await waitForCalls(model, 2);\\n  expect(JSON.stringify(pending[1].request)).toContain(\\\"OTHER_PRIVATE_EVIDENCE\\\");\\n  expect(JSON.stringify(pending[1].request)).not.toMatch(/ALPHA_BATTERY_EVIDENCE|Alpha bakery|a-review|OMIT_/);\\n  pending[1].resolve(\\\"Other user's authorized answer\\\");\\n  expect(await other).toBe(true);\\n  pending[0].resolve(\\\"Prior user private answer\\\");\\n  expect(await alpha).toBe(false);\\n  expect(session.getSnapshot().result?.value).toBe(\\\"Other user's authorized answer\\\");\\n});\\n'@\\n[IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $destRoot 'tests/unit/services/qa/s2018-context-prompt-compatibility.test.ts'), $imports + $prefix + $tail, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))\\nnpm.cmd test -- --runTestsByPath tests/unit/services/qa/s2018-context-prompt-compatibility.test.ts\",\"workdir\":\"<USER_HOME>\\\\Documents\\\\NJIT\\\\Fall 26\\\\CS490\\\\ReviewLens-Codex-Artifacts\\\\reviewlens-s2-010-prompt-66cee59\",\"sandbox_permissions\":\"require_escalated\",\"justification\":\"May I test the S2-018 adapter against actual S2-010/S2-013 modules in the detached compatibility worktree, keeping unmerged dependencies out of the feature branch?\",\"yield_time_ms\":1000,\"max_output_tokens\":2000}));\n",

### 2026-11-12T23:53:50.644Z ? assistant

The request session and representative React tests now pass. Switching to B clears A’s visible answer, and a delayed A response cannot replace B’s result—even if cancellation is ignored. Five isolated tests using the real context and prompt builders also passed, covering fresh target/user context and rejection before the controlled model call for foreign, empty, or oversized datasets.

### 2026-11-12T23:55:40.714Z ? custom_tool_call

````json
{
  "type": "custom_tool_call",
  "id": "ctc_0e4d266f1c69a697016af651d18a7887d19575ec81fb57a558",
  "status": "completed",
  "call_id": "call_0b02902d3e5f4861b3ef347ce73e84c9",
  "name": "exec",
  "input": "text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:\"$taskFile = Join-Path (Get-Location) 'docs/s2-018-target-context-switching.md'\\n$taskContent = @'\\n# S2-018: Change Q&A context when the target changes\\n\\nIssue: [#69](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/issues/69).\\nOwner: Shawn. Independent implementation is ready for workspace integration; the\\ncomplete story remains open until the actual UI, summary and Q&A endpoint are wired.\\n\\n## Architecture and boundaries\\n\\nThe branch is based independently on main `f931a34`. It adds no dependency,\\nprovider call, migration, route or replacement workspace. Melissa owns workspace\\nand target-selection UI; Aryan owns Q&A orchestration; Bansi owns summary data.\\n\\nTwo reusable modules implement the local lifecycle:\\n\\n- `src/lib/qa/target-request-coordinator.ts`: correlates requests with a target,\\n  UI session and unique request ticket; invalidates previous work immediately.\\n- `src/lib/qa/target-question-session.ts`: observable request adapter around the\\n  coordinator. It invokes an injected transport, publishes safe snapshots and\\n  rejects stale successes/errors without passing raw exceptions to the UI.\\n\\nS2-010 independently authenticates every context read and checks target ownership.\\nA local UI session key is not authentication and must never contain a JWT, cookie,\\npassword or provider credential. Use a non-secret account/session epoch that\\nchanges on sign-out or account changes. Never create a module-level singleton.\\n\\n## Integration API for Melissa and Aryan\\n\\n```ts\\nconst questions = createTargetQuestionSession<ValidatedQaReply>({\\n  execute: postQuestionForTarget,\\n});\\n\\nquestions.selectTarget({ targetId: activeTarget.id, sessionKey: uiSessionEpoch });\\nawait questions.ask(question);\\n```\\n\\n`ValidatedQaReply` and `postQuestionForTarget` above stand for the actual shared\\nresponse type and decoder/transport; they are not new endpoint exports or an\\ninvented HTTP schema. The transport contract is:\\n\\n```ts\\ninterface TargetQuestionRequest {\\n  readonly targetId: string;\\n  readonly question: string;\\n  readonly signal: AbortSignal;\\n}\\n\\nexecute(request: TargetQuestionRequest): Promise<{\\n  readonly targetId: string;\\n  readonly value: ValidatedQaReply;\\n}>;\\n```\\n\\nThe adapter submits only the captured target, trimmed question and AbortSignal.\\nIt does not submit browser-owned reviews, owner IDs, prior answers, provider\\nconversation IDs or a local session key. Apply the agreed question-size limit\\nat the UI and server; this lifecycle module only rejects blank questions.\\n\\nThe transport must validate the real response before returning `value`. Obtain\\nresponse target identity from the server's validated response/request binding;\\n**do not blindly relabel an arbitrary response with the current target ID**. A\\ncurrent response with the wrong target is discarded and becomes safe error state.\\n\\nThe server must authenticate/authorize afresh, invoke S2-010 for that request's\\ntarget, and pass its typed target/reviews into S2-013. Do not append the serialized\\nevidence again or reuse another target's provider history. S2-011 must enforce\\nthe complete model budget and validate the model reply/citations.\\n\\n| Session method | Behavior |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| `selectTarget(scope)` | Clears old answers/errors and aborts pending work when target/session changes |\\n| `selectTarget(null)` | Clears selection and invalidates work on logout/deselection |\\n| `ask(question)` | Starts latest-request-wins work using the captured target; resolves true only for an accepted result |\\n| `getSnapshot()` | Stable current `{ scope, status, result }` for rendering |\\n| `subscribe(listener)` | Observes snapshot changes; returns unsubscribe function |\\n| `cancel()` | Clears pending/result state while retaining selection |\\n| `dispose()` | Clears/aborts permanently and releases subscribers; safe to call repeatedly |\\n\\nInvalid usage (blank question, asking without a selection, using a disposed\\ninstance) rejects before the transport runs. Network/context/provider failures\\nproduce `{ status: \\\"error\\\", result: null }`, with no arbitrary error text stored.\\n`ask()` returns only an acceptance boolean; render `getSnapshot()` rather than a\\nraw transport promise result. Stale errors/cleanup do not reset newer loading state.\\n\\n### React integration checklist\\n\\n1. Create one session for a workspace lifetime, with a stable transport. The\\n   component owning that lifetime also owns its cleanup.\\n2. Subscribe with `useSyncExternalStore(questions.subscribe,\\n   questions.getSnapshot, questions.getSnapshot)`; snapshots stay stable until\\n   state changes. The third argument is appropriate for a client-owned session\\n   with the same initial state; do not share user state across SSR requests.\\n3. Change selection synchronously in the target-selection action **before**\\n   rendering the newly active target. Do not wait for an asynchronous effect to\\n   hide the previous answer. A keyed workspace can also isolate lifetimes.\\n4. Identify the active target visibly. Bind summary loading/results to the same\\n   target/session; summary data is owned separately and must also reject stale\\n   completion. This adapter neither loads nor calculates summaries.\\n5. Render only the session's accepted result, and clear question draft if the UI\\n   design requires it. Any answer category must obey the same correlation checks.\\n6. Pass the captured AbortSignal to fetch. Cancellation is best effort: the\\n   adapter rejects late responses even if the transport ignores it. Aborting a\\n   browser request does not prove that paid server/model work was cancelled.\\n7. On logout, select null immediately; on account change use a new session epoch.\\n   On final workspace teardown dispose the session. React Strict Mode replays\\n   effects: create a fresh instance for a fresh lifetime rather than reusing a\\n   disposed one during effect setup/cleanup replay.\\n\\nThe lower-level coordinator remains available for independently correlated\\nsummary/loading adapters. Tickets are opaque objects: comparing target IDs alone\\ncannot distinguish old A from fresh A during A-to-B-to-A transitions.\\n\\n## Tests and evidence\\n\\nCommitted-source candidate tests (in this feature worktree):\\n\\n- `target-request-coordinator.test.ts`: 22 cases covering invalidation, A/B/A,\\n  same-target supersession, scope copying, isolated instances, account changes,\\n  abort ordering, wrong-target replies, cancellation, disposal and stale errors.\\n- `target-question-session.test.ts`: 17 cases covering the injected transport,\\n  exact submission fields, subscriptions, reversed completion, safe errors/retry,\\n  logout/account switching, synchronous failures and subscriber-triggered changes.\\n- `target-question-session-ui.test.tsx`: three React Testing Library cases using\\n  a representative consumer. They verify visible target/fixture-summary switching,\\n  immediate removal of an old answer, safe mismatch feedback and late responses.\\n  This consumer is test-only, not Melissa's production UI or Bansi's summary.\\n\\nSeparate compatibility evidence lives outside the repository in the detached\\n`reviewlens-s2-010-prompt-66cee59` worktree under `ReviewLens-Codex-Artifacts`:\\n\\n- Actual S2-010 source from `c9c7f385a8355264491d93366ce36f2635ae75b9`.\\n- Actual S2-013 source from `66cee597d46daf792df23a8a881bb8f2dfba7a18`.\\n- This session adapter plus a controlled model consumer and mocked Supabase I/O.\\n- Five passing cases inspect the complete outgoing prompt request after target\\n  and account switching, reject late old answers, and prove no controlled model\\n  call for foreign, empty or oversized context.\\n\\nThose probes do not change the unmerged dependency branches or import unavailable\\nmodules into this main-based branch. The first probe run had a misplaced Jest\\nNode-environment header and failed in jsdom; moving the header to the first line\\nresolved the harness error and all five passed. No live model or hosted data was used.\\n\\n## Remaining story acceptance\\n\\nMelissa's UI and Aryan's published endpoint were not available at this checkpoint.\\nWire the adapter into their actual flow; make the real target/summary/answer agree;\\nport the compatibility scenarios to S2-003's shared harness; and run browser tests\\nwith real switching, reversed completion, account changes and failure cases.\\nS2-019 still needs its full browser/server/model/cache stale-context audit.\\n\\nKeep `Refs #69`, not `Closes #69`, until this integration is demonstrated. Normal\\nreview, independent security review, author/manual verification and published-head\\nCI remain required. No claim is made that an isolated adapter completes the story.\\n\\n## Validation\\n\\nThe final completion checkpoint below records checks run on this worktree. Older\\nfoundation results (667 tests/48 suites, lint/typecheck/build and 100% coordinator\\ncoverage) preceded the request adapter and are not current-branch CI evidence.\\n\\nAI assistance: Codex. Available original task dialogue, failures and corrections\\nare retained in `ai-transcripts/2026-11-12_shawn_S2-018_codex.md` with privacy notes.\\n'@\\n[IO.File]::WriteAllText($taskFile, $taskContent, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))\\nnpx.cmd prettier --write docs/s2-018-target-context-switching.md\",\"workdir\":\"<USER_HOME>\\\\Documents\\\\NJIT\\\\Fall 26\\\\CS490\\\\ReviewLens-Codex-Artifacts\\\\reviewlens-s2-018-target-context\",\"sandbox_permissions\":\"require_escalated\",\"justification\":\"May I update S2-018’s handoff documentation with the implemented adapter contract and exact remaining integration checks?\",\"yield_time_ms\":1000,\"max_output_tokens\":1200}));\n",
  "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {

    "create_time": 1794527697.5475118

}
````

### 2026-11-12T23:55:43.803Z ? user

1. Hey Shawn,  I'm good with the core architecture: &#x20;
   - Server-only `buildReviewDatasetContext(targetId)` with authentication and ownership verified internally.
   - Using only persisted reviews belonging to the authenticated user's active target.
   - Building fresh context for every question, without cross-request caching.
   - Excluding reviewer names, arbitrary metadata, and ingestion details.
   - Treating review text as untrusted evidence rather than system instructions.
   - Failing safely on empty, oversized, or unavailable datasets without silently truncating reviews.
   **A few integration points I'd like us to finalize:** &#x20;
   1. **Model boundary:** Aryan should expose an injectable model adapter so Deep and I can capture the complete outgoing model request. My S2-016 tests need to verify that another user's reviews, IDs, or metadata never reach the model, even when the question attempts to reveal them.
   - Missing versus foreign targets returning indistinguishable errors.
   - No model invocation after any authorization or context-building failure.
   - No Alpha private-review marker appearing anywhere in User B's outgoing model request or answer.
   If we agree on these points, I can develop the final S2-016 integration tests against the approved interfaces without waiting for the entire Q&A implementation to merge. The architecture looks good from my side. Thanks!
2.

2) **Evidence limits:** I'm okay with the proposed 100-review / 32-KiB limits as initial safeguards, provided exceeding either limit produces an explicit failure and no model invocation. Aryan and Shawn should agree on the full token budget, including instructions, question, evidence, and reserved output.

3. **Response contract:** I support your proposed error codes as a starting point. Let's align with our existing `{ success, error: { code, message } }` format and have Aryan and Melissa confirm the final HTTP mappings and corresponding UI states.

&#x20;For S2-016 integration, I'd also like our tests to cover:&#x20;

- User B requesting User A's target.
- Spoofed user/owner identifiers.
- Consecutive requests across different users and targets.







Hey Shawn, thanks for reviewing PR #73 and running the additional compatibility checks.\
\
I reviewed your feedback, and I agree with both integration adjustments.\
\
For S2-016 Phase 2:\
\
We'll call your actual buildReviewDatasetContext(targetId) and mock the underlying session/authentication and Supabase infrastructure, rather than passing a user identity into the builder or mocking away its ownership checks.\
\
For foreign-target requests, we'll allow the builder to execute its authorization checks, but assert that no review lookup occurs, no context is successfully returned, and no model invocation happens.\
\
Once S2-011 is available, we'll capture the entire outgoing model request and verify cross-user isolation, previous-target exclusion, and authorized review evidence.\
\
I'll update the S2-016 integration documentation to reflect your contract and keep the existing seven Phase 1 tests unchanged.\
\
After that, could you re-review the documentation update? We'll keep PR #73 in draft until the full AI-boundary tests are implemented and passing.\
\
Thanks for checking compatibility with S2-010!
